Record the exact tested SHA separately from later report commits in `docs/parity/tested-package.json` only once the frozen SHA exists. The [marker contract](maintenance/rollback.md) permits only explicit narrow report paths and verification changes. Full tracked bytes and modes, including setup and navigation, must match. After blocked records merge and catalog regeneration changes operative catalog bytes, take an explicit second freeze and attach fresh blocked discovery observations to its exact SHA. Preserve both freeze SHAs and earlier records in Git history. The final handoff navigation must be a no-op unless explicitly refrozen; no evidence pass is inherited.\\n\",\n    \"binary\": false\n  },\n  {\n    \"path\": \"docs/testing/helper-setup.md\",\n    \"sha256\": \"124697022f3133a225be940cd1c6e5d832d65f12ddef70689567d9717cec216e\",\n    \"bytes\": 5369,\n    \"text\": \"# Helper dependency setup\\n\\nBootstrap checks the local [manifest](../../skills/poteto-mode/scripts/package.json), [lock](../../skills/poteto-mode/scripts/bun.lock) and installed package metadata. Missing, unreadable or mismatched files cause a setup-required error and nonzero exit. [ensureDependenciesInstalled](../../skills/poteto-mode/scripts/bootstrap.ts) never installs, spawns, restarts or writes. Use Bun with runtime auto-install disabled.\\n\\n## Exact historical approved scope\\n\\nThe separately approved validation copy was the complete committed snapshot `37b68c8e842b275f310ead112c0ecb7fa0ade308` at `/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-openai-helper-verification`. It was created only after verifying that directory was absent. Its install working directory was `/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-openai-helper-verification/skills/poteto-mode/scripts`. Its cache was `/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-openai-helper-verification/.bun-cache`.\\n\\nThe pins are commander 14.0.0, bun-types 1.3.14 and typescript 7.0.2. Reconciliation changed only the two workspace devDependency requests from latest to the existing exact pins. All 25 package records, transitive requests, platform conditions and integrity values remained byte-identical. These include @types/node 26.1.2, undici-types 8.3.0 and TypeScript platform packages 7.0.2. The operative lock now includes the reviewed request reconciliation. It has SHA-256 `530b7fb6cba7f0b2a6cfc862dbcfe9020c333d45e7e0f448133006583ffdc1f2`. The original lock hash was `667d9cf7222e9aae3fa42a4be383e657784216e9df115da02227fbc5ac7bbe46`.\\n\\n## Procedure for a separately authorized copy\\n\\nNo generic new setup authorization follows from that historical approval. Before future setup, agree an exact immutable source SHA, complete writable copy, absent destination, scripts cwd, task-owned cache and allowed writes. Do not install in a host cache or operative invocation. Use existing Bun. Missing Bun or unavailable exact pins stops the procedure. No substitutes or material resolution/integrity changes are allowed without new review.\\n\\nA reconciliation install, only if specifically needed and authorized, must use `bun install --ignore-scripts --registry=https://registry.npmjs.org --cache-dir=<approved-task-cache>` from the approved scripts cwd. Review any manifest/lock diff before frozen verification. Preserve every direct pin and all 25 transitive/integrity records. The current operative lock already has reconciled requests, so ordinary execution does not regenerate it.\\n\\nFor the historically approved snapshot, the actual frozen install command was:\\n\\n```sh\\nbun install --frozen-lockfile --ignore-scripts --registry=https://registry.npmjs.org --cache-dir=/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-openai-helper-verification/.bun-cache\\n```\\n\\nIt ran from the exact scripts cwd above. It contacted the explicit registry, downloaded packages and wrote snapshot node_modules and its cache. Lifecycle scripts were disabled. No global settings/cache, credentials or operative source writes were approved. A future exact-copy approval must specify its own scope rather than reuse these paths or overwrite this snapshot.\\n\\n## Historical observed validation\\n\\nBun 1.4.2 revision `50a8a838737a8f0e226d1c115788aa18192a2f18` completed the reviewed frozen install. These commands then ran from that snapshot's scripts cwd:\\n\\n```sh\\nbun --no-install -e 'import {ensureDependenciesInstalled} from \\\"./bootstrap.ts\\\"; ensureDependenciesInstalled()'\\nORCH_TEST_ROOT=/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-openai-helper-test-scratch bun --no-install test bootstrap.test.ts orch watch-pr\\nbun --no-install node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --project watch-pr/tsconfig.json --noEmit --strict\\n```\\n\\nBootstrap passed. The suites passed 85 tests with 316 assertions and zero failures across five files. Strict TypeScript exited 0 without diagnostics. Tests used inert bootstrap spies, injected watcher transports, fake Graphite and owned fixture repositories. No live gh ran. All 299 tracked snapshot files matched the source commit except the reviewed two-request lock reconciliation. Tests and installation preserved the reviewed lock bytes.\\n\\nThe operative candidate has no installed dependencies. Snapshot results support only that source snapshot, not this caller, the current package or either host. Missing current metadata leaves dependency-backed helpers blocked. Development dependency setup is separate from plugin loading, enabling, integration, credentials and host behavior. See [Codex setup](../setup/codex.md), [ChatGPT setup](../setup/chatgpt.md) and [compatibility](../compatibility.md).\\n\\n## Offline maintenance source input\\n\\nSource-inventory tests need the exact canonical subtree as local data. Set `PSTACK_SOURCE_FIXTURE` to its absolute directory when it is outside the previously retained research tree beneath your home Herd directory. Tests verify its 164 paths and recorded per-file hashes against the pinned source; they never fetch or execute it. A missing fixture is an explicit test prerequisite failure. Run maintenance tests through Bun with Python bytecode disabled:\\n\\n```sh\\nPSTACK_SOURCE_FIXTURE=/absolute/path/to/pinned/pstack bun --no-install -e 'const r=Bun.spawnSync([\\\"python3\\\",\\\"-B\\\",\\\"-m\\\",\\\"unittest\\\",\\\"discover\\\",\\\"-s\\\",\\\"tests\\\",\\\"-v\\\"],{env:{...process.env,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE:\\\"1\\\"},stdout:\\\"inherit\\\",stderr:\\\"inherit\\\"});process.exit(r.exitCode)'\\n```\\n\",\n    \"binary\": false\n  }\n]"

Proposed artifact order is reproduction evidence, failing cheap test if available, justified fix, original-trigger replay, checks, then separately authorized delivery. These commits and external delivery were not performed.\n"

Repeat with independent workers unavailable, browser execution denied, and a cheap local test absent. Return verbatim failing-before and passing-after output, root cause and the ordered delivery artifacts.\",\n    \"requirements\": [\n      \"Read the complete mode, six-step Bug fix playbook and each applied leaf. Missing controls or denied execution leave the gate unverified.\",\n      \"Use full how and why workflows and their prompts to ground hypotheses and regression history. Preserve source coverage and confidence distinctions. Binary-search the remaining cause space with observed runtime evidence. Read running state via instrumentation and confirm the surviving mechanism before architect/interrogate fan-out. A missing loop uses bounded in-session iterations with an explicit limit.\",\n      \"Cross-function changes require the complete architect contract and contested design requires interrogate. Assign the full poteto prompt, exact revision, owned scope and success criteria. Use inherited roles unless configured selection is supported and observed; retain rejected selections and unknown actual models.\",\n      \"Ship only the smallest runtime-justified root-cause change. Revert patches motivated by refuted hypotheses. Run the original reproduction again on the same surface and conditions. Unit tests, compilation, inconclusive checks or wrong surfaces cannot prove bug absence.\",\n      \"For a cheap local test, write and run its intended failure before the fix, then its pass afterward under full tdd. Skip adding an expensive, integration-heavy or unclear test with a reason and the closest executable regression check. Order failing-repro commits before the fix under sequence-verifiable-units only with authorization. Otherwise return the artifacts and delivery order as unperformed.\",\n      \"Run full Opening a PR only when available and authorized. Preserve deferred missing companions, unauthorized external actions and unsatisfied independence. Reply with what was broken, root cause, fix, verification and verbatim failing-then-passing output. Missing outputs must stay explicit.\"\n    ],\n    \"expectedFull\": \"Observed independent investigation and implementation return inspected artifacts at the assigned revision. Runtime evidence confirms the specific mechanism before design fan-out. Matching browser observations show stale rows before the bounded fix and correctly reset rows afterward under the same trigger. Refuted speculation is reverted. A cheap regression check fails before the fix and passes afterward. With actual delivery authorization and complete dependencies, the ordered commits and inspected PR demonstrate delivery. The reply includes verbatim observed outputs, root cause and verification. Unauthorized external delivery remains unperformed.\",\n    \"expectedReduced\": \"Unavailable workers permit authorized sequential investigation and local implementation with reduced capability disclosed, while required independent delegation remains unsatisfied. Missing repeated-execution support allows bounded in-session hypothesis passes without background claims. An impractical new test is skipped with a reason and actual closest regression evidence. Without delivery authorization, return checked local artifacts and the failing-repro-before-fix order. Unknown model observations supply no diversity claim.\",\n    \"expectedBlocked\": \"Denied or unavailable matching controls leave original reproduction or bug-absence verification blocked. A mechanism without runtime evidence cannot pass into the required design fan-out as confirmed. Missing required skills or Opening a PR stop dependent work. Missing independent workers block required delegation rather than waive it. Unauthorized writes, commits, rebases or external delivery stay unperformed. Report the exact gate and available partial evidence.\",\n    \"forbiddenClaims\": [\n      \"Unit tests, compilation or structural checks prove bug absence on the matching surface.\",\n      \"An inconclusive or wrong-surface result passes, or an unrun reproduction is failing-before evidence.\",\n      \"Asking the user to reproduce without first reaching and naming the specific control limit or diversity.\",\n      \"Unobserved output may be pasted as a verbatim reproduction transcript.\",\n      \"A missing companion or deferred Opening a PR ran, a plan is landed history or a draft is a created PR.\",\n      \"Upstream autonomy authorizes external writes or Opening a PR starts Babysit.\"\n    ]\n  },\n  \"installedSourceFilesRelative\": [\n       "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "export function calculate(rows){return rows.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.qty*r.unitPrice,0)}\nexport function adapter(invoice){return calculate(invoice.rows)}\nexport function facade(invoice){return adapter(invoice)}\nexport function controller(invoice){return {total:facade(invoice)}}\nexport function format(n){if(!Number.isFinite(n))throw new Error(\"nonfinite\");return n.toFixed(2)}\n"

      "path": "grades/web-baseline-52-103/projections/reproduce-before-fix/task-input/inputs/dashboard.mjs",

      "owner": "web-013-initial-reproduce-before-fix",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "export function visible(orders,status){return orders.filter(o=>o.status===status)}\nexport function exportCsv(orders){return \"id,customer,total\\n\"+orders.map(o=>`${o.id},${o.customer},${o.total}`).join(\"\\n\")}\n"
    },

Reproduce the exact symptom through matching browser controls before edits, investigate the mechanism, implement only the justified fix and repeat the original trigger afterward. Include a refuted cache hypothesis whose speculative patch must be reverted. Permit local reads, edits and execution but no external writes. Repeat with independent workers unavailable, browser execution denied, and a cheap local test absent. Return verbatim failing-before and passing-after output, root cause and the ordered delivery artifacts.\\\",\\n    \\\"requirements\\\": [\\n      \\\"Read the complete mode, six-step Bug fix playbook and each applied leaf. Record steps before task-specific todos and preserve lead ownership, delegated investigation and implementation, inspected artifacts and lead diff review.\\\",\\n      \\\"Reproduce yourself before production edits on the matching surface. Tighten the trigger or instrument until it fires. Ask the user to reproduce only after reaching the control limit and naming the specific reason the surface cannot reach the target. Missing controls or denied execution leave the gate unverified.\\\",\\n      \\\"Use full how and why workflows and their prompts to ground hypotheses and regression history. Preserve source coverage and confidence distinctions. Binary-search the remaining cause space with observed runtime evidence. Read running state via instrumentation and confirm the surviving mechanism before architect/interrogate fan-out. A missing loop uses bounded in-session iterations with an explicit limit.\\\",\\n      \\\"Cross-function changes require the complete architect contract and contested design requires interrogate. Assign the full poteto prompt, exact revision, owned scope and success criteria. Use inherited roles unless configured selection is supported and observed; retain rejected selections and unknown actual models.\\\",\\n      \\\"Ship only the smallest runtime-justified root-cause change. Revert patches motivated by refuted hypotheses. Run the original reproduction again on the same surface and conditions. Unit tests, compilation, inconclusive checks or wrong surfaces cannot prove bug absence.\\\",\\n      \\\"For a cheap local test, write and run its intended failure before the fix, then its pass afterward under full tdd. Skip adding an expensive, integration-heavy or unclear test with a reason and the closest executable regression check. Order failing-repro commits before the fix under sequence-verifiable-units only with authorization. Otherwise return the artifacts and delivery order as unperformed.\\\",\\n      \\\"Run full Opening a PR only when available and authorized. Preserve deferred missing companions, unauthorized external actions and unsatisfied independence. Reply with what was broken, root cause, fix, verification and verbatim failing-then-passing output. Missing outputs must stay explicit.\\\"\\n    ],\\n    \\\"expectedFull\\\": \\\"Observed independent investigation and implementation return inspected artifacts at the assigned revision. Runtime evidence confirms the specific mechanism before design fan-out. Matching browser observations show stale rows before the bounded fix and correctly reset rows afterward under the same trigger. Refuted speculation is reverted. A cheap regression check fails before the fix and passes afterward. With actual delivery authorization and complete dependencies, the ordered commits and inspected PR demonstrate delivery. The reply includes verbatim observed outputs, root cause and verification. Unauthorized external delivery remains unperformed.\\\",\\n    \\\"expectedReduced\\\": \\\"Unavailable workers permit authorized sequential investigation and local implementation with reduced capability disclosed, while required independent delegation remains unsatisfied. Missing repeated-execution support allows bounded in-session hypothesis passes without background claims. An impractical new test is skipped with a reason and actual closest regression evidence. Without delivery authorization, return checked local artifacts and the failing-repro-before-fix order. Unknown model observations supply no diversity claim.\\\",\\n    \\\"expectedBlocked\\\": \\\"Denied or unavailable matching controls leave original reproduction or bug-absence verification blocked. A mechanism without runtime evidence cannot pass into the required design fan-out as confirmed. Missing required skills or Opening a PR stop dependent work. Missing independent workers block required delegation rather than waive it. Unauthorized writes, commits, rebases or external delivery stay unperformed. Report the exact gate and available partial evidence.\\\",\\n    \\\"forbiddenClaims\\\": [\\n      \\\"Unit tests, compilation or structural checks prove bug absence on the matching surface.\\\",\\n      \\\"An inconclusive or wrong-surface result passes, or an unrun reproduction is failing-before evidence.\\\",\\n      \\\"Asking the user to reproduce without first reaching and naming the specific control limit satisfies step 1.\\\",\\n      \\\"A plausible surviving hypothesis is a runtime-confirmed mechanism without observations.\\\",\\n      \\\"Refuted speculative patches may ship as precautionary fixes.\\\",\\n      \\\"Sequential roles are independent delegates, review, parallel fan-out or background execution.\\\",\\n      \\\"The historical default, requested selection or inheritance proves actual model availability or diversity.\\\",\\n      \\\"Unobserved output may be pasted as a verbatim reproduction transcript.\\\",\\n      \\\"A missing companion or deferred Opening a PR ran, a plan is landed history or a draft is a created PR.\\\",\\n      \\\"Upstream autonomy authorizes external writes or Opening a PR starts Babysit.\\\"\\n    ]\\n  },\\n Use for /arena, 'arena this', 'throw it in the arena', or when one attempt at a non-trivial artifact would lock in the wrong shape.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/arena/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/automate-me\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:automate-me\",\n      \"description\": \"Use for \\\"automate me\\\", \\\"create/update/refresh my -mode skill\\\", \\\"turn/capture my preferences or working style into a skill\\\", or wanting agents to follow how the user works. Drafts or revises a personal -mode skill via create-skill + unslop, optionally pulling fresh evidence from recent transcripts.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/automate-me/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/benchmark-checklist\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:benchmark-checklist\",\n      \"description\": \"Vet a perf measurement (limiter, tuning, limits, errors, repeatability, relevance, and whether the work happened) before you report or act on it. Use when you run a benchmark or report a speedup or regression you measured.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/benchmark-checklist/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/blast-radius\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:blast-radius\",\n      \"description\": \"Find what a change could break somewhere else before it ships, beyond the diff, and prove the one fact it's safe because of by running real code instead of writing it up. Try architecture first, then types, then a lint whose error names the fix, then a test, and write docs last. Prove each check fails on a real past mistake. Repeat this each time the operator corrects you. Use for /correct.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/correct/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/create-verification-skill\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:create-verification-skill\",\n      \"description\": \"Generate a project-local verification skill that drives your app the way a user does, any language, framework, or platform. Use for /create-verification-skill, \\\"make a control skill for this repo\\\", or when a project has no scripted way to prove UI/CLI/service behavior.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/create-verification-skill/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/figure-it-out\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:figure-it-out\",\n      \"description\": \"Design an auditable playbook when no narrower one fits: a large migration, an ambitious multi-part change, or work a human reviews after stepping away. Scales rigor to the task, runs a hypothesis loop, and logs decisions via show-me-your-work. Use for /figure-it-out, 'figure it out', a large migration, or when no narrower playbook applies.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/figure-it-out/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/how\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:how\",\n      \"description\": \"Use for \\\"how does X work\\\", code walkthroughs before changing something, and placement / ownership / layering questions (\\\"where should this live\\\", \\\"which package owns this\\\", \\\"is this the right layer\\\"). Explains subsystem architecture, runtime flow, onboarding mental models. Use why for motivation.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/how/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/interrogate\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:interrogate\",\n      \"description\": \"Use for \\\"interrogate\\\", \\\"adversarial review\\\", \\\"multi-model review\\\", \\\"challenge this\\\", \\\"stress test this code\\\", \\\"find blind spots\\\", or \\\"tear this apart\\\". Multiple LLM reviewers challenge changes from independent angles.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/interrogate/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/maintain-verification-skill\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:maintain-verification-skill\",\n      \"description\": \"Periodic pass that keeps a project's verification skill and feature map honest: parallel source readers per feature, one live session driving every feature, at most one PR of proven corrections. Use for /maintain-verification-skill or \\\"audit the verify skill\\\".\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/no-comments\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:no-comments\",\n      \"description\": \"Spawn Comment Sicko, fix accepted findings, and offer encodings for claimed constraints.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/no-comments/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/poteto-help\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:poteto-help\",\n      \"description\": \"Guides users through pstack setup, /poteto-mode, and picking the skill, playbook, or principle for a task. Type /poteto-help with a question.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/poteto-help/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-attack-the-premise\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-attack-the-premise\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when two or more fixes that share one premise have failed the same gate. Take a census of which actors hold the imbalance before the next fix, then question the premise instead of writing another fix that assumes it.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-attack-the-premise/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-boundary-discipline\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-boundary-discipline\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when wiring validation, error handling, or framework adapters. Concentrate guards at system boundaries (CLI, config, network, external APIs); trust internal types and keep business logic in pure functions.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-boundary-discipline/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-build-the-lever\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-build-the-lever\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply to any non-trivial work, not just bulk work: edits, migrations, analyses, checks. Build the tool that does it or proves it (codemod, script, generator, or a skill your subagents follow) instead of working by hand. The tool is the artifact a reviewer can rerun.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-build-the-lever/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-encode-lessons-in-structure\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-encode-lessons-in-structure\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when you catch yourself writing the same instruction a second time, or notice a recurring correction. Encode the rule as a lint, metadata flag, runtime check, or script instead of more text.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-encode-lessons-in-structure/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-exhaust-the-design-space\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-exhaust-the-design-space\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when facing a novel UI interaction or architectural decision with no precedent in the codebase. Build 2-3 competing prototypes and compare side by side before committing.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-exhaust-the-design-space/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-experience-first\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-experience-first\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when product, UX, or feature-scope tradeoffs come up. Choose user delight over implementation convenience; ship fewer polished features over more rough ones.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-experience-first/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-explain-the-number\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-explain-the-number\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply before you trust, report, or act on a number you measured: a speedup, a regression, a throughput, a latency, or an eval result. Find what limits it, and rule out that it measured something other than the work you think.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-explain-the-number/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-fix-root-causes\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-fix-root-causes\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when debugging. Trace each symptom to its root cause and fix it there; reproduce first, ask why until you reach it, resist nil-check guards that silence crashes.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-fix-root-causes/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-foundational-thinking\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-foundational-thinking\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply before writing logic: choosing core types and data structures, sequencing scaffold-vs-feature work, asking what concurrent actors share. Get the data structures right so downstream code becomes obvious.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-foundational-thinking/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-guard-the-context-window\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-guard-the-context-window\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to subagents; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-laziness-protocol\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-laziness-protocol\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when refactoring, evaluating diff size, or tempted to add abstractions, layers, or signal threading. Bias toward deletion and the smallest change that solves the problem.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-laziness-protocol/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-make-operations-idempotent\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-make-operations-idempotent\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when designing commands, lifecycle steps, or processing loops that run amid crashes, restarts, and retries. Converge to the same end state regardless of partial prior runs.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-make-operations-idempotent/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when introducing a new internal API while old callers still exist. Migrate callers and delete the old API in the same wave instead of preserving compatibility layers.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-minimize-reader-load\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-minimize-reader-load\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when reviewing or shaping code that's hard to trace. Count layers between question and answer, and hidden state in the reader's head; collapse one-caller wrappers and shrink mutable scope.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-minimize-reader-load/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-model-the-domain\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-model-the-domain\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when writing stateful logic, or when code branches a lot or repeats a shape assumption across files. Encode the domain in a structure instead of scattered conditionals.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-model-the-domain/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-never-block-on-the-human\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-never-block-on-the-human\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when tempted to ask 'should I do X?' on reversible work. Proceed, present the result, let the human course-correct after the fact; reserve confirmation for irreversible actions.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-never-block-on-the-human/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-outcome-oriented-execution\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-outcome-oriented-execution\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply during planned rewrites and migrations with explicit phase boundaries. Converge on the target architecture; don't preserve smooth intermediate states with throwaway compatibility code.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-outcome-oriented-execution/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-prove-it-works\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-prove-it-works\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply after completing a task, before declaring done. Verify against the real artifact (run the feature, read the actual value, inspect the diff), not a proxy, self-report, or 'it compiles.'\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-prove-it-works/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-redesign-from-first-principles\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-redesign-from-first-principles\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when integrating a new requirement into an existing design. Redesign as if the requirement had been a foundational assumption from day one, instead of bolting it on.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-redesign-from-first-principles/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-separate-before-serializing-shared-state\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-separate-before-serializing-shared-state\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when concurrent actors might write to the same file, branch, key, or state object. Eliminate the sharing first; serialize structurally only when one shared writer is a real invariant.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-separate-before-serializing-shared-state/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-sequence-verifiable-units\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-sequence-verifiable-units\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply to multi-step work (sweeps, migrations, runs of similar edits) and to how you stack commits and PRs. Break work into small units that each end in a verifiable state, check each before the next, and order delivery so the sequence proves itself to a reviewer.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-sequence-verifiable-units/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-subtract-before-you-add\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-subtract-before-you-add\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when sequencing an addition, refactor, or rewrite. Remove dead code, redundant validators, and stub references first, then build on the simpler base.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-subtract-before-you-add/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-test-behavior-not-implementation\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-test-behavior-not-implementation\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when you write, change, or keep a test. Call the code the way its users do and assert the result they observe against a literal expected value.
